     minBound = nodeMin;\n        maxBound = nodeMax;\n        halfExtent = (maxBound - minBound) * 0.5f;\n        center = (minBound + maxBound) * 0.5f;\n\n        // If few enough triangles or too small, make this a leaf\n        if (indices.size() <= MAX_OCTREE_TRIS ||\n            (maxBound - minBound).norm() < MAX_OCTREE_LEAF_SIZE) {\n            triangleIndices = indices;\n ? childHalfSize.z : -childHalfSize.z;\n\n                if (triangleBoxOverlap(childCenter, childHalfSize, tri)) {\n                    childIndices[i].push_back(idx);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Check if we can't split further (all triangles in one child)\n        bool canSplit = false;\n        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {\n            if (!childIndices[i].empty() (std::abs(c.y) > halfExtent.y + ad.y) return false;\n        if (std::abs(c.z) > halfExtent.z + ad.z) return false;\n\n        if (std::abs(d.y * c.z - d.z * c.y) > halfExtent.y * ad.z + halfExtent.z halfExtent.x * ad.y + halfExtent.y * ad.x + EPSILON) return false;\n\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    // Apply a function to all triangles potentially intersecting the ray\n    // Returns true if the function returns true for any triangle\n    template<typename Func>\n    bool applyToTris(const Vec3& p1, const Vec3& p2, Func&& func) const {\n        // If leaf node, check triangles directly\n        if (!triangleIndices.empty()) {\n            for (size_t idx : triangleIndices) {\n                if (func(idx, (*allTriangles)[idx])) return true;\n            }\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        // Otherwise, descend to children\n        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {\n            if (children[i] && children[i]->rayIntersectsBox(p1, p2)) {\n                if (children[i]->applyToTris(p1, p2, func)) return true;\n            }\n        }\n        return false;\n    }\n};\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Mesh Generation: Icosphere\n// ============================================================================\n\n// Generate an icosphere by subdividing an icosahedron\nclass IcosphereMesh {\npublic:\n    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;\n\n    IcosphereMesh(int subdivisions, val_t radius) {\n        // Initial icosahedron vertices\n        const val_t t = (1.0f + std::sqrt(5.0f)) / 2.0f;\n\n radius,\n            Vec3( 0,  1, -t).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( t,  0, -1).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( t,  0,  1).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3(-t,  0,            {0, 11, 5}, {0, 5, 1}, {0, 1, 7}, {0, 7, 10}, {0, 10, 11},\n            {1, 5, 9}, {5, 11, 4}, {11, 10, 2}, {10, 7, 6}, {7, 1, 8},\n            {3, 9, 4}, {3, 4, 2}, {3, 2, 6}, {3, 6, 8}, std::vector<std::array<idx_t, 3>> newFaces;\n            std::map<std::pair<idx_t, idx_t>, idx_t> midpointCache;\n\n            auto getMidpoint = [&](idx_t i1, idx_t i2) -> idx_t {\n                auto key = std::make_pair(std::min(i1, i2), std::max(i1, i2));\n                auto it = midpointCache.find(key);\n                if (it != midpointCache.end()) return it->second;\n\n                Vec3 mid = (vertices[i1] + vertices[i2]) / 2.0f;\n                mid = mid.normalized() * radius;\n                idx_t idx = static_cast<idx_t>(vertices.size());\n                vertices.push_back(mid);\n                midpointCache[key] = idx;\n                return idx;\n            };\n\n            for (const auto& face : faces) {\n                idx_t a = std::move(newFaces);\n        }\n\n        // Build triangles (normals pointing inward for a \"room\")\n        triangles.reserve(faces.size());\n        for (const auto& face : faces) {\n            // Reverse winding to make normals point inward\n            triangles.emplace_back(vertices[face[2]], vertices[face[1]], vertices[face[0]]);\n        }\n    }\n};\n\n// RandomGenerator {\n    std::mt19937 rng;\n    std::uniform_real_distribution<val_t> dist;\npublic:\n    explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f) {}\n    val_t rand() v1, const Vec3& v2) {\n    Vec3 e1 = v1 - v0;\n    Vec3 e2 = v2 - v0;\n    Vec3 pvec = dir.cross(e2);\n    val_t det = e1.dot(pvec);\n\n    if (std::abs(det) < EPSILON) return return true;  // Ray is blocked\n        }\n        return false;\n    });\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Form Factor Computation (Kij)\n//  val_t vNorm = v.norm();\n    if (vNorm <= EPSILON) return ZERO;\n    return std::max(ZERO, v.dot(normal) / vNorm);\n}\n\n// Compute form factor Kij between triangle i (receiver) and triangle j (emitter)\nval_t computeKij(size_t idxI, size_t idxJ,\n                  const std::vector<Triangle>& triangles,\n                  const Octree& octree,\n                  RandomGenerator& rng) {\n    const Triangle& triI = triangles[idxI];\n    const Triangle& triJ = triangles[idxJ];\n\n    // Cull triangles facing the same direction\n    if (triI.normal().dot(triJ.normal()) > 0.99f) return ZERO;\n\n    val_t kij = ZERO;\n\n    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {\n        Vec3 pI = randomPointInTriangle(triI, rng);\n        Vec3 pJ = randomPointInTriangle(triJ, rng);\n\n        if (isRayBlocked(pI, pJ, octree, idxI, idxJ)) continue;\n\n        Vec3 v = pJ - pI;\n        val_t distSqr = v.squaredNorm();\n        if (distSqr < EPSILON) continue;\n\n        val_t cosPhiI = cosPhi(v, triI.normal());\n        val_t cosPhiJ = cosPhi(-v, triJ.normal());\n\n        if (cosPhiI <= ZERO || cosPhiJ <= ZERO) continue;\n\n        kij += (cosPhiI * cosPhiJ) / (PI * distSqr);\n    }\n\n    return kij * INV_NUM_RAYS;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Tau (time delay) Computation\n// ============================================================================\n\nint computeTau(const Triangle& triI, const Triangle& triJ) {\n    val_t dist = (triI.center() - triJ.center()).norm();\n    return static_cast<int>(std::ceil(dist * INV_WAVE_SPEED));\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Room Response Simulation State\n// ============================================================================\n\nstruct SimulationState {\n    size_t numTriangles;\n    size_t numTimesteps;\n\n    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;\n    std::vector<val_t> areas;       // Area of each triangle\n    std::vector<val_t> rho;         // Reflectivity (0.0 to 1.0)\n    std::vector<val_t> kij;         // Form factors (N x N matrix, row-major)\n    std::vector<int> tau;           // Time delays (N x N matrix, row-major)\n    std::vector<val_t> radE;        // Emission radiosity (T x N matrix)\n    std::vector<val_t> radB;        // Reflected radiosity (T x N matrix)\n    std::vector<val_t> distances;   // Computed distances from source\n\n    Octree octree;                  // Spatial acceleration structure\n\n    size_t sourceIndex;\n\n    size_t idx2d(size_t i, size_t j) const { return i * numTriangles + j; }\n    size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }\n};\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Initialization\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid initializeSimulation(SimulationState& state, int subdivisions, size_t timesteps,\n                          size_t sourceIdx, val_t reflectivity) {\n    // Generate mesh\n    IcosphereMesh mesh(subdivisions, 10.0f);  // Radius 10 units\n    state.triangles = std::move(mesh.triangles);\n    state.numTriangles = state.triangles.size();\n    state.numTimesteps = timesteps;\n    state.sourceIndex = sourceIdx % state.numTriangles;\n\n    printf(\"Generated icosphere mesh with %zu triangles\\n\", state.numTriangles);\n\n    // Build octree for spatial acceleration\n    printf(\"Building octree...\\n\");\n    state.octree.build(state.triangles);\n\n    // Initialize areas\n    state.areas.resize(state.numTriangles);\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        state.areas[i] = state.triangles[i].area();\n    }\n\n    // Initialize reflectivity\n    state.rho.resize(state.numTriangles, reflectivity);\n\n    // Initialize matrices\n    state.kij.resize(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n    state.tau.resize(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles, 0);\n    state.radE.resize(timesteps * state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n    state.radB.resize(timesteps * state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n    state.distances.resize(state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n\n    // Set source emission (active for first half of timesteps)\n    size_t timeOn = 0;\n    size_t timeOff = timesteps / 2;\n    for (size_t t = timeOn; t < timeOff; ++t) {\n        state.radE[state.idxTN(t, state.sourceIndex)] = 1.0f;\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Precomputation Phase\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {\n    printf(\"Computing form factors (Kij)...\\n\");\n    RandomGenerator rng(42);\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {\n            if (i == j) continue;\n            state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)] = computeKij(\n                i, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rng);\n        }\n        if ((i + 1) % 100 == 0 || i + 1 == state.numTriangles) {\n            printf(\"  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\\n\", i + 1, state.numTriangles);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nvoid computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& state) {\n    printf(\"Computing time delays (Tau)...\\n\");\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {\n            if (i == j) continue;\n            state.tau[state.idx2d(i, j)] = computeTau(\n                state.triangles[i], state.triangles[j]);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid runSimulation(SimulationState& state) {\n    printf(\"Running wave propagation simulation...\\n\");\n\n    for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n            val_t sumB = ZERO;\n\n            for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {\n                if (i == j) continue;\n\n                int tauij = state.tau[state.idx2d(i, j)];\n\n                // Skip if wave hasn't yet propagated from j to i\n                if (static_cast<int>(t) < tauij) continue;\n\n                val_t kij = state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)];\n                if (kij <= ZERO) continue;\n\n                // Get radiosity from source triangle at time when emission occurred\n                size_t srcTime = t - static_cast<size_t>(tauij);\n                val_t radJ = state.radB[state.idxTN(srcTime, j)];\n                if (radJ <= ZERO) continue;\n\n                // Accumulate contribution: form factor * area * source radiosity\n                sumB += std::min(kij * state.areas[j], ONE) * radJ;\n            }\n\n            // Update radiosity: reflection + emission\n            state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] = state.rho[i] * sumB + state.radE[state.idxTN(t, i)];\n        }\n\n        if ((t + 1) % 10 == 0 || t + 1 == state.numTimesteps) {\n            printf(\"  Timestep %zu/%zu\\n\", t + 1, state.numTimesteps);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Distance Computation (Cross-Correlation)\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid computeDistances(SimulationState& state) {\n    printf(\"Computing distances via cross-correlation...\\n\");\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        val_t maxCorr = ZERO;\n        int bestT = 0;\n\n        // Discrete cross-correlation to find time delay\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {\n            val_t sum = ZERO;\n\n            for (size_t tt = t; tt < state.numTimesteps; ++tt) {\n                val_t pB = state.radB[state.idxTN(tt, i)];\n                val_t pS = state.radB[state.idxTN(tt - t, state.sourceIndex)];\n                sum += pS * pB;\n            }\n\n            if (sum > maxCorr) {\n                maxCorr = sum;\n                bestT = static_cast<int>(t);\n            }\n        }\n\n        state.distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Validation\n// ============================================================================\n\nbool validateResults(const SimulationState& state) {\n    printf(\"\\nValidation:\\n\");\n\n    // Check that distances are non-negative\n    bool allNonNegative = true;\n    val_t minDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    val_t maxDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t sumDist = ZERO;\n    int nonZeroCount = 0;\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        val_t d = state.distances[i];\n        if (d < 0) {\n            allNonNegative = false;\n            printf(\"  ERROR: Negative distance at triangle %zu: %f\\n\", i, d);\n        }\n        if (!std::isfinite(d)) {\n            printf(\"  ERROR: Non-finite distance at triangle %zu: %f\\n\", i, d);\n            return false;\n        }\n        minDist = std::min(minDist, d);\n        maxDist = std::max(maxDist, d);\n        sumDist += d;\n        if (d > EPSILON) nonZeroCount++;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"  Distance range: [%.4f, %.4f]\\n\", minDist, maxDist);\n    printf(\"  Average distance: %.4f\\n\", sumDist / static_cast<val_t>(state.numTriangles));\n    printf(\"  Non-zero distances: %d/%zu\\n\", nonZeroCount, state.numTriangles);\n\n    // Check source distance is zero or very small\n    val_t srcDist = state.distances[state.sourceIndex];\n    if (srcDist > WAVE_SPEED * 2) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Source triangle distance is non-zero: %.4f\\n\", srcDist);\n    }\n\n    // Check radiosity propagation (some triangles should have received energy)\n    int receivedEnergy = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {\n            if (state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] > EPSILON) {\n                receivedEnergy++;\n                break;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    printf(\"  Triangles receiving energy: %d/%zu\\n\", receivedEnergy, state.numTriangles);\n\n    if (receivedEnergy == 0) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: No triangles received energy - simulation failed\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Check Kij matrix (should have some non-zero entries)\n    int nonZeroKij = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        if (state.kij[i] > EPSILON) nonZeroKij++;\n    }\n    printf(\"  Non-zero form factors: %d/%zu (%.2f%%)\\n\",\n           nonZeroKij, state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles,\n           100.0f * nonZeroKij / static_cast<val_t>(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles));\n\n    if (nonZeroKij == 0) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: All form factors are zero - visibility computation failed\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    if (!allNonNegative) {\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Hash for Verification\n// ============================================================================\n\nuint64_t computeHash(const SimulationState& state) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        const uint32_t* ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint32_t*>(&state.distances[i]);\n        hash ^= (static_cast<uint64_t>(*ptr) + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\n//       subdivisions++;\n        triangles *= 4;\n    }\n    return subdivisions;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Main\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Target number of triangles (default: 320)\\n\");\n    printf(\"               Actual count will be rounded to nearest icosphere level:\\n\");\n    printf(\"               20, 80, 320, 1280, 5120, 20480\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -t <num>     Number of timesteps (default: 50)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Source triangle index (default: {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-fable-5-1","id":"msg_011CgZVEKezw34kcwGXBSSPG","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01WDFiu9gjYN78APtsJD9CUY","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"cat > /tmp/tail.cpp <<'EOF'\n// ============================================================================\n// Room Response Simulation State (row-distributed across MPI ranks)\n// ============================================================================\n\n// Number of random draws consumed by computeKij for a pair that is not culled\nconstexpr uint64_t DRAWS_PER_PAIR = static_cast<uint64_t>(NUM_RAYS) * 4;\n\nstruct SimulationState {\n    size_t numTriangles;\n    size_t numTimesteps;\n\n    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;\n    std::vector<val_t> areas;       // Area of each triangle (replicated)\n    std::vector<val_t> rho;         // Reflectivity (replicated)\n\n    // Row distribution: this rank owns rows [rowBegin, rowEnd) of the N x N matrices\n    size_t rowBegin = 0;\n    size_t rowEnd = 0;\n    size_t numLocalRows = 0;\n    std::vector<int> rowCounts;     // Rows per rank\n    std::vector<int> rowDispls;     // First row per rank\n    uint64_t rngDrawOffset = 0;     // rand() draws consumed before this rank's first row\n\n    std::vector<val_t> kij;         // Form factors, local rows (numLocalRows x N, row-major).\n                                    // After precomputation, holds min(kij * area[j], 1).\n    std::vector<int> tau;           // Time delays, local rows (numLocalRows x N, row-major)\n    std::vector<val_t> radE;        // Emission radiosity (T x N matrix, replicated)\n    std::vector<val_t> radB;        // Reflected radiosity (T x N matrix, replicated)\n    std::vector<val_t> distances;   // Computed distances from source (N, replicated)\n\n    long long nonZeroKij = 0;       // Global count of form factors > EPSILON\n\n    Octree octree;                  // Spatial acceleration structure (replicated)\n\n    size_t sourceIndex;\n\n    int rank = 0;\n    int size = 1;\n\n    size_t idxLocal(size_t li, size_t j) const { return li * numTriangles + j; }\n    size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }\n};\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Row Partitioning (load balanced by number of ray-traced pairs)\n// ============================================================================\n\n// A pair (i, j) is ray traced iff it is not culled by the normal test in computeKij.\nstatic inline bool pairIsTraced(const Triangle& triI, const Triangle& triJ) {\n    return !(triI.normal().dot(triJ.normal()) > 0.99f);\n}\n\nvoid partitionRows(SimulationState& state) {\n    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;\n    const int size = state.size;\n    const int rank = state.rank;\n\n    // Step 1: count traced pairs per row, distributed with a naive block split\n    std::vector<int> blkCounts(size), blkDispls(size);\n    for (int r = 0; r < size; ++r) {\n        size_t lo = n * static_cast<size_t>(r) / size;\n        size_t hi = n * static_cast<size_t>(r + 1) / size;\n        blkDispls[r] = static_cast<int>(lo);\n        blkCounts[r] = static_cast<int>(hi - lo);\n    }\n    std::vector<long long> rowWeights(n, 0);\n    {\n        size_t lo = blkDispls[rank];\n        size_t hi = lo + blkCounts[rank];\n        for (size_t i = lo; i < hi; ++i) {\n            long long c = 0;\n            const Triangle& ti = state.triangles[i];\n            for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n                if (i == j) continue;\n                c += pairIsTraced(ti, state.triangles[j]) ? 1 : 0;\n            }\n            rowWeights[i] = c;\n        }\n    }\n    MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, MPI_DATATYPE_NULL,\n                   rowWeights.data(), blkCounts.data(), blkDispls.data(), MPI_LONG_LONG,\n                   MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    // Step 2: prefix sums and balanced cuts. Each row also carries a small base cost\n    // (the culling loop itself), so weight = traced pairs + 1.\n    std::vector<long long> prefixTraced(n + 1, 0);\n    std::vector<long long> prefixCost(n + 1, 0);\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        prefixTraced[i + 1] = prefixTraced[i] + rowWeights[i];\n        prefixCost[i + 1] = prefixCost[i] + rowWeights[i] + 1;\n    }\n    const long long total = prefixCost[n];\n\n    std::vector<size_t> cuts(size + 1, 0);\n    cuts[size] = n;\n    for (int r = 1; r < size; ++r) {\n        long long target = (total * r) / size;\n        size_t pos = static_cast<size_t>(\n            std::lower_bound(prefixCost.begin(), prefixCost.end(), target) - prefixCost.begin());\n        pos = std::min(pos, n);\n        cuts[r] = std::max(cuts[r - 1], pos);\n    }\n\n    state.rowCounts.resize(size);\n    state.rowDispls.resize(size);\n    for (int r = 0; r < size; ++r) {\n        state.rowDispls[r] = static_cast<int>(cuts[r]);\n        state.rowCounts[r] = static_cast<int>(cuts[r + 1] - cuts[r]);\n    }\n    state.rowBegin = cuts[rank];\n    state.rowEnd = cuts[rank + 1];\n    state.numLocalRows = state.rowEnd - state.rowBegin;\n    state.rngDrawOffset = static_cast<uint64_t>(prefixTraced[state.rowBegin]) * DRAWS_PER_PAIR;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Initialization\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid initializeSimulation(SimulationState& state, int subdivisions, size_t timesteps,\n                          size_t sourceIdx, val_t reflectivity) {\n    // Generate mesh (deterministic, replicated on every rank)\n    IcosphereMesh mesh(subdivisions, 10.0f);  // Radius 10 units\n    state.triangles = std::move(mesh.triangles);\n    state.numTriangles = state.triangles.size();\n    state.numTimesteps = timesteps;\n    state.sourceIndex = sourceIdx % state.numTriangles;\n\n    if (state.rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Generated icosphere mesh with %zu triangles\\n\", state.numTriangles);\n        printf(\"Building octree...\\n\");\n    }\n    state.octree.build(state.triangles);\n\n    // Initialize areas\n    state.areas.resize(state.numTriangles);\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        state.areas[i] = state.triangles[i].area();\n    }\n\n    // Initialize reflectivity\n    state.rho.resize(state.numTriangles, reflectivity);\n\n    // Distribute matrix rows across ranks\n    partitionRows(state);\n    if (state.rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Distributed %zu rows over %d MPI ranks (rank 0 owns %zu rows)\\n\",\n               state.numTriangles, state.size, state.numLocalRows);\n    }\n\n    // Initialize matrices (local rows only for the N x N ones)\n    state.kij.assign(state.numLocalRows * state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n    state.tau.assign(state.numLocalRows * state.numTriangles, 0);\n    state.radE.assign(timesteps * state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n    state.radB.assign(timesteps * state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n    state.distances.assign(state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n\n    // Set source emission (active for first half of timesteps)\n    size_t timeOn = 0;\n    size_t timeOff = timesteps / 2;\n    for (size_t t = timeOn; t < timeOff; ++t) {\n        state.radE[state.idxTN(t, state.sourceIndex)] = 1.0f;\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Precomputation Phase\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {\n    if (state.rank == 0) printf(\"Computing form factors (Kij)...\\n\");\n\n    // Reproduce the sequential RNG stream: skip to the position of our first row.\n    RandomGenerator rng(42);\n    const uint64_t callsPerDraw = RandomGenerator::engineCallsPerDraw();\n    rng.discardEngine(state.rngDrawOffset * callsPerDraw);\n\n    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;\n    long long localNonZero = 0;\n\n    for (size_t li = 0; li < state.numLocalRows; ++li) {\n        const size_t i = state.rowBegin + li;\n        val_t* row = &state.kij[state.idxLocal(li, 0)];\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            if (i == j) continue;\n            val_t k = computeKij(i, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rng);\n            row[j] = k;\n            if (k > EPSILON) ++localNonZero;\n        }\n        if (state.rank == 0 && ((li + 1) % 100 == 0 || li + 1 == state.numLocalRows)) {\n            printf(\"  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles (rank 0 share)\\n\", li + 1, state.numLocalRows);\n        }\n    }\n\n    MPI_Allreduce(&localNonZero, &state.nonZeroKij, 1, MPI_LONG_LONG, MPI_SUM, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    // Fold the per-timestep weight min(kij * area[j], 1) into the matrix once.\n    // Entries with kij <= 0 stay <= 0 so the simulation can skip them identically.\n    for (size_t li = 0; li < state.numLocalRows; ++li) {\n        val_t* row = &state.kij[state.idxLocal(li, 0)];\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            val_t k = row[j];\n            row[j] = (k <= ZERO) ? ZERO : std::min(k * state.areas[j], ONE);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nvoid computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& state) {\n    if (state.rank == 0) printf(\"Computing time delays (Tau)...\\n\");\n\n    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;\n    for (size_t li = 0; li < state.numLocalRows; ++li) {\n        const size_t i = state.rowBegin + li;\n        int* row = &state.tau[state.idxLocal(li, 0)];\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            if (i == j) continue;\n            row[j] = computeTau(state.triangles[i], state.triangles[j]);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid runSimulation(SimulationState& state) {\n    if (state.rank == 0) printf(\"Running wave propagation simulation...\\n\");\n\n    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;\n\n    for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {\n        const int ti = static_cast<int>(t);\n        val_t* radBt = &state.radB[state.idxTN(t, 0)];\n\n        for (size_t li = 0; li < state.numLocalRows; ++li) {\n            const size_t i = state.rowBegin + li;\n            const val_t* wRow = &state.kij[state.idxLocal(li, 0)];\n            const int* tauRow = &state.tau[state.idxLocal(li, 0)];\n            val_t sumB = ZERO;\n\n            for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n                if (i == j) continue;\n\n                int tauij = tauRow[j];\n\n                // Skip if wave hasn't yet propagated from j to i\n                if (ti < tauij) continue;\n\n                val_t w = wRow[j];\n                if (w <= ZERO) continue;\n\n                // Get radiosity from source triangle at time when emission occurred\n                size_t srcTime = t - static_cast<size_t>(tauij);\n                val_t radJ = state.radB[state.idxTN(srcTime, j)];\n                if (radJ <= ZERO) continue;\n\n                // Accumulate contribution: min(form factor * area, 1) * source radiosity\n                sumB += w * radJ;\n            }\n\n            // Update radiosity: reflection + emission\n            radBt[i] = state.rho[i] * sumB + state.radE[state.idxTN(t, i)];\n        }\n\n        // Share this timestep's radiosity row with all ranks (tau >= 1 for i != j,\n        // so timestep t only depends on earlier timesteps).\n        MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, MPI_DATATYPE_NULL,\n                       radBt, state.rowCounts.data(), state.rowDispls.data(), MPI_FLOAT,\n                       MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        if (state.rank == 0 && ((t + 1) % 10 == 0 || t + 1 == state.numTimesteps)) {\n            printf(\"  Timestep %zu/%zu\\n\", t + 1, state.numTimesteps);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Distance Computation (Cross-Correlation)\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid computeDistances(SimulationState& state) {\n    if (state.rank == 0) printf(\"Computing distances via cross-correlation...\\n\");\n\n    const size_t T = state.numTimesteps;\n\n    // Contiguous copies of the time series (same arithmetic, better locality)\n    std::vector<val_t> src(T), col(T);\n    for (size_t tt = 0; tt < T; ++tt) src[tt] = state.radB[state.idxTN(tt, state.sourceIndex)];\n\n    for (size_t li = 0; li < state.numLocalRows; ++li) {\n        const size_t i = state.rowBegin + li;\n        for (size_t tt = 0; tt < T; ++tt) col[tt] = state.radB[state.idxTN(tt, i)];\n\n        val_t maxCorr = ZERO;\n        int bestT = 0;\n\n        // Discrete cross-correlation to find time delay\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < T; ++t) {\n            val_t sum = ZERO;\n            for (size_t tt = t; tt < T; ++tt) {\n                sum += src[tt - t] * col[tt];\n            }\n            if (sum > maxCorr) {\n                maxCorr = sum;\n                bestT = static_cast<int>(t);\n            }\n        }\n\n        state.distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);\n    }\n\n    MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, MPI_DATATYPE_NULL,\n                   state.distances.data(), state.rowCounts.data(), state.rowDispls.data(),\n                   MPI_FLOAT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Validation (rank 0; distances and radB are replicated, Kij count is reduced)\n// ============================================================================\n\nbool validateResults(const SimulationState& state) {\n    printf(\"\\nValidation:\\n\");\n\n    // Check that distances are non-negative\n    bool allNonNegative = true;\n    val_t minDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    val_t maxDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t sumDist = ZERO;\n    int nonZeroCount = 0;\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        val_t d = state.distances[i];\n        if (d < 0) {\n            allNonNegative = false;\n            printf(\"  ERROR: Negative distance at triangle %zu: %f\\n\", i, d);\n        }\n        if (!std::isfinite(d)) {\n            printf(\"  ERROR: Non-finite distance at triangle %zu: %f\\n\", i, d);\n            return false;\n        }\n        minDist = std::min(minDist, d);\n        maxDist = std::max(maxDist, d);\n        sumDist += d;\n        if (d > EPSILON) nonZeroCount++;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"  Distance range: [%.4f, %.4f]\\n\", minDist, maxDist);\n    printf(\"  Average distance: %.4f\\n\", sumDist / static_cast<val_t>(state.numTriangles));\n    printf(\"  Non-zero distances: %d/%zu\\n\", nonZeroCount, state.numTriangles);\n\n    // Check source distance is zero or very small\n    val_t srcDist = state.distances[state.sourceIndex];\n    if (srcDist > WAVE_SPEED * 2) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Source triangle distance is non-zero: %.4f\\n\", srcDist);\n    }\n\n    // Check radiosity propagation (some triangles should have received energy)\n    int receivedEnergy = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {\n            if (state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] > EPSILON) {\n                receivedEnergy++;\n                break;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    printf(\"  Triangles receiving energy: %d/%zu\\n\", receivedEnergy, state.numTriangles);\n\n    if (receivedEnergy == 0) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: No triangles received energy - simulation failed\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Check Kij matrix (should have some non-zero entries)\n    size_t totalEntries = state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles;\n    printf(\"  Non-zero form factors: %lld/%zu (%.2f%%)\\n\",\n           state.nonZeroKij, totalEntries,\n           100.0f * static_cast<val_t>(state.nonZeroKij) / static_cast<val_t>(totalEntries));\n\n    if (state.nonZeroKij == 0) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: All form factors are zero - visibility computation failed\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    if (!allNonNegative) {\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Hash for Verification\n// ============================================================================\n\nuint64_t computeHash(const SimulationState& state) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        uint32_t bits;\n        std::memcpy(&bits, &state.distances[i], sizeof(bits));\n        hash ^= (static_cast<uint64_t>(bits) + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Helper: Compute subdivision level from target triangle count\n// ============================================================================\n\nint getSubdivisionsForTriangleCount(int targetTriangles) {\n    // Icosphere: 20 triangles initially, 4x per subdivision\n    // subdivisions: 0->20, 1->80, 2->320, 3->1280, 4->5120, 5->20480\n    int subdivisions = 0;\n    int triangles = 20;\n    while (triangles < targetTriangles && subdivisions < 6) {\n        subdivisions++;\n        triangles *= 4;\n    }\n    return subdivisions;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Main\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Target number of triangles (default: 320)\\n\");\n    printf(\"               Actual count will be rounded to nearest icosphere level:\\n\");\n    printf(\"               20, 80, 320, 1280, 5120, 20480\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -t <num>     Number of timesteps (default: 50)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Source triangle index (default: 0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r <val>     Reflectivity 0.0-1.0 (default: 0.8)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -o           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\n// Wall-clock milliseconds for a phase, synchronized across all ranks\nstatic long phaseMillis(double start, double end) {\n    return static_cast<long>((end - start) * 1000.0);\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n    int rank = 0, size = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &size);\n\n    int targetTriangles = 320;\n    int timesteps = 50;\n    int sourceIdx = 0;\n    val_t reflectivity = 0.8f;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments (identical on all ranks)\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            targetTriangles = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-t\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            timesteps = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            sourceIdx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            reflectivity = static_cast<val_t>(atof(argv[++i]));\n        } == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: getSubdivisionsForTriangleCount(targetTriangles);\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Room Response Simulation Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"===================================\\n\");\n        printf(\"Target triangles: %d (using %d subdivisions)\\n\", targetTriangles, subdivisions);\n        printf(\"Timesteps: %d\\n\", timesteps);\n        printf(\"Source triangle: %d\\n\", sourceIdx);\n        printf(\"Reflectivity: %.2f\\n\", reflectivity);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n        printf(\"MPI ranks: %d\\n\", size);\n        printf(\"\\n\");\n    }\n\n    // Initialize\n    SimulationState state;\n    state.rank = rank;\n    state.size = size;\n    initializeSimulation(state, subdivisions, MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    double startPre = MPI_Wtime();\n\n    computeTimeDelays(state);\n    computeFormFactors(state);\n\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    double endPre = static_cast<double>(n * t * t);\n\n        printf(\"\\nPerformance:\\n\");\n        printf(\"  Triangles: %zu\\n\", n);\n        printf(\"  Timesteps: %zu\\n\", t);\n        printf(\"  MPI ranks: size_t memKij = n * n * sizeof(val_t);\n        size_t memTau = n * n * sizeof(int);\n        size_t memRad = 2 * t * n * sizeof(val_t) * static_cast<size_t>(size);\n        size_t totalMem = memKij + memTau + memRad;\n        printf(\"  Memory usage: %.2f MB\\n\", totalMem / (1024.0 * 1024.0));\n\n        // Hash\n        uint64_t hash = computeHash(state);\n        printf(\"  Result hash: %016lX\\n\", hash);\n        printf(\"\\n\");\n\n        // Print results for external validation\n        if (printResults) {\n            // Convert distances to double for output\n            std::vector<double> distData(state.distances.begin(), state.distances.end());\n            print_results(distData, \"Distances\");\n        }\n\n        // Validation\n        if (validate) {\n            if (!validateResults(state)) {\n                exitCode = 1;\n            }\n        }\n        fflush(stdout);\n    }\n\n    MPI_Bcast(&exitCode, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n The partition is load-balanced by the number of ray-traced pairs per row, computed cheaply up front from the normal-culling test.\n- **Exact RNG reproduction.** The original uses one sequential Mersenne Twister across all pairs. Each rank seeds identically and skips ahead to the exact stream position of its first row. The number of engine calls per draw is detected at runtime rather than assumed, so the skip is robust to the standard library's implementation.\n- **Wave propagation.** Each rank computes its rows for timestep t, then a single in-place allgather shares that timestep's radiosity row. Since every delay is at least one step, there is no intra-timestep dependency. The per-timestep weight min(kij·area, 1) is now folded into the matrix once instead of recomputed every step, which performs the same float operations in the same order.\n- **Distance correlation** is split by the same rows, uses contiguous copies of the time series for locality, and the results are allgathered. Validation and hashing happen on rank 0 with the non-zero form-factor count reduced across ranks.
